# Ezekiel Decoded — Full Edition
### A Hebrew–Morse decoding reading of Ezekiel, verse by verse

**Nature of this document.** This is a text edition with recomputable numerical annotation and a seat-layer reading. It is not a new revelation, not an independent factual verification, and not a claim to add to or subtract from the canon.

**Source text.** Westminster Leningrad Codex (WLC), consonantal text. Vowels, accents, spaces, maqaf and sof pasuq are not counted. Ketiv/qere variants follow the qere (read) form; standalone s/pe paragraph markers are not counted.

**English text.** King James Version (public domain), aligned to the Hebrew verse numbering. Where English and Hebrew numbering differ (Ezekiel 20–21), the KJV reference is given in the verse line.

## 1. Counting rules

1. Only Hebrew consonants are counted; **G** = the sum of the letter values.
2. Final forms take their standard values: **ך=20, ם=40, ן=50, ף=80, ץ=90** — not 500–900.
   Check: תַּחְלִיף ("substitute") = 400+8+30+10+80 = **528**, matching the source example. Counting ף as 800 gives 1248, which does not match.
   *Note on the repository's main README:* its gematria table follows the earlier convention (final forms 500–900). This Ezekiel edition follows the 2026-08-20 correction. The two conventions differ only for final forms; the method is otherwise unchanged.
3. **m14** = G mod 14; **m7** = G mod 7; the digital root is the repeated digit sum.
4. The **seat** is m14 on the fourteen-seat table; the **cluster** is named by (m7, m14).
5. Checks: prime/composite and factorization; mod 42 (= 3×14); mod 49 (= 7×7); joint checks +441 (truth) and +600 (deceiver). The verse lines keep the 42/49 remainders so every value can be recomputed.
6. Because this volume starts from the Hebrew text, steps 2–3 of the nine-step method (Chinese back to Hebrew, Morse transliteration) do not apply; the remaining steps are executed on the source text.

## 2. Four rules (read before the numbers)

1. Decoding is lossy: revelation may be without error; decoding is not. This edition is one family of checkable, lossy readings.
2. Seat is not value: a seat is a structural coordinate, not a moral verdict. Landing at 12 does not mean 'false'; landing at 8 does not mean 'resurrection.'
3. Same number is not same identity: one value can point in opposite directions. The test is oath and fruit, not the number. Examples: גאל (redeem) and בבל (Babylon) are both 34; חטא (sin) and חי (live) are both 18.
4. No identification of persons: this edition does not identify any person or organization as 'Antichrist' or 'false prophet.' The drama of a vision cannot replace the four checks of text, Christology, fruit, and the framework's own limits.

## 3. The fourteen seats

| m14 | Seat | Note |
|:---:|------|------|
| 0 | Zero / Davidic Covenant | inside the covenant, zeroed; time prophecies (70/490/1260/42) land here |
| 1 | Unique | prime double-one = uniqueness given by God; composite double-one = self-multiplied, self-certifying, usurping |
| 2 | Joseph | suffering -> exaltation -> saving a remnant; supply comes from outside |
| 3 | One Step from the Gate | activity of the aerial powers; the instruction cluster |
| 4 | Gate | entrance, passage; the true name / Messiah son of David 424 stands here |
| 5 | I / Void | the false-prophet seat; the void points toward resurrection |
| 6 | Man / Babel | the human level, the Babel project; the millennium also lands here |
| 7 | Truth | truth, half-completion; resurrection words gather here |
| 8 | Resurrection | resurrection; counterfeit resurrection also sits here |
| 9 | Suffering / Law | suffering, law, the pain of judgment |
| 10 | Costume / Love-Neighbor | a garment that can be put on and taken off; love your neighbor as yourself |
| 11 | False Light / Void | false light, ineffectiveness; where tool and truth are joined |
| 12 | Deceiver | deception, title, identity, false witness |
| 13 | Refusal / Love | refusal and love share the seat; the signature of eternal fire |

**Clusters by (m7, m14):**

- Double Zero · Covenant/Zero ← m7=0 ∧ m14=0
- Double One · Unique seat ← m7=1 ∧ m14=1
- Double Two · Joseph seat ← m7=2 ∧ m14=2
- Double Three · Instruction cluster ← m7=3 ∧ m14=3
- Double Four · Double gate ← m7=4 ∧ m14=4
- Double Five · Prophet modulus ← m7=5 ∧ m14=5
- Double Six · Battlefield/Beast ← m7=6 ∧ m14=6
- I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7) ← m7=0 ∧ m14=7
- Love-fire fork (m7=6 & m14=13) ← m7=6 ∧ m14=13

Caveat: by definition m7 = m14 mod 7, so a "double X" is a naming convention, not a second independent test. Seats 0–7 and 13 always carry a cluster name; seats 8–12 do not. A seat and a cluster must not be presented as double verification.

## Chapter 1 — The Four Living Creatures and the Throne of Glory

**Chapter total:** G=105090 · m14=6 Man / Babel · m7=6 · root=6 · composite 2×3×5×31×113 · 42→6 · 49→34
**Seat distribution:** 10 Costume × 5 · 11 False Light × 4 · 13 Refusal/Love × 4 · 0 Zero × 3 · 7 Truth × 3 · 5 I/Void × 2 · 8 Resurrection × 2 · 12 Deceiver × 2 · 3 One-step × 1 · 4 Gate × 1 · 6 Man/Babel × 1 (28 verses)
**Special clusters:** Love-fire fork (m7=6 & m14=13): 5, 8, 9, 18 (4) · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero: 4, 19, 21 (3) · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7): 12, 25, 27 (3) · Double Five · Prophet modulus: 3, 10 (2) · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast: 6 (1) · Double Three · Instruction cluster: 11 (1) · Double Four · Double gate: 20 (1)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **רוח** (ruach, wind / spirit) — G=214 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=7 · composite 2×107 · Double Four · Double gate
- **כסא** (kisse, throne) — G=81 · m14=11 False Light / Void · m7=4 · root=9 · composite 3^4
- **כבוד** (kavod, glory) — G=32 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=5 · composite 2^5 · Double Four · Double gate
- **אש** (esh, fire) — G=301 · m14=7 Truth · m7=0 · root=4 · composite 7×43 · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7)
- **דמות** (demut, likeness / form) — G=450 · m14=2 Joseph · m7=2 · root=9 · composite 2×3^2×5^2 · Double Two · Joseph seat
- **אופן** (ofan, wheel) — G=137 · m14=11 False Light / Void · m7=4 · root=2 · prime

**Reading**

The opening vision stacks wind, fire, living creatures, wheels, expanse, throne and glory. At the seat layer, רוח (214) and כבוד (32) both land at the gate (4, double four), while דמות (450) lands at Joseph (2); but כסא (81) and אופן (137) both land at 11 (false light). This does not make the throne false — judgment 4 holds that a landing point is not a value. What can be read is structural: the chapter gives the gate to wind, spirit and glory; throne and wheels are only the vehicles that are seen. The climax of the vision is not a throne one can possess but a glory that is arriving.

## Chapter 2 — The Scroll, the Mouth, and the House of Rebellion

**Chapter total:** G=33275 · m14=11 False Light / Void · m7=4 · root=2 · composite 5^2×11^3 · 42→11 · 49→4
**Seat distribution:** 4 Gate × 2 · 10 Costume × 2 · 1 Unique × 1 · 2 Joseph × 1 · 3 One-step × 1 · 5 I/Void × 1 · 6 Man/Babel × 1 · 8 Resurrection × 1 (10 verses)
**Special clusters:** Double Four · Double gate: 3, 4 (2) · Double One · Unique seat: 2 (1) · Double Three · Instruction cluster: 5 (1) · Double Five · Prophet modulus: 7 (1) · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast: 8 (1) · Double Two · Joseph seat: 9 (1)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **לב** (lev, heart) — G=32 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=5 · composite 2^5 · Double Four · Double gate
- **רוח** (ruach, wind / spirit) — G=214 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=7 · composite 2×107 · Double Four · Double gate
- **נביא** (navi, prophet) — G=63 · m14=7 Truth · m7=0 · root=9 · composite 3^2×7 · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7)
- **דבר** (davar, word / matter / pestilence) — G=206 · m14=10 Costume / Love-Neighbor · m7=3 · root=8 · composite 2×103

**Reading**

Scroll, eating, mouth, heart, a house of rebellion. לב (32) and רוח (214) land at the gate (4); נביא (63) lands at truth (7) and carries the I AM signature (m7=0); דבר (206) lands at the costume seat (10). The prophet's identity stands on the signature, while the word itself sits in a garment that can be put on and taken off. The tension of the chapter: the mouth is opened, but the heart is still the battlefield of rebellion.

**Verse-by-verse**

**Reading**

Watchman, silence, blood-guilt. צפה (175) lands at truth (7) with the signature; דם (44) at Joseph (2, double two); עון (126) at zero (0, double zero). The watchman's warning is not a prophecy show but a transfer of blood-guilt: if he does not speak, the blood is charged to him; if he speaks, it is charged to those who will not hear. Zero is not whitewash; it puts iniquity back on the covenant ledger.

**Reading**

Brick, iron plate, lying on one side, famine, bread by weight. ירושלם (586) and בשר (502) both land at 12 (deceiver); עון (126) and מצור (336) both land at 0 (zero); לחם (78) at 8 (resurrection); מים (90) at 6 (man). Siege and iniquity share the zero: judgment happens inside the covenant, not outside it. City and flesh share 12 — a structural position that can be besieged and weighed, not a moral verdict on its people.

**Reading**

The sword is at zero, the fire at truth: the sword of judgment returns to the covenant's starting point, while the fire stands on the signature. Of the three divisions, the part that survives is not luck; it is placed into the wind.

**Reading**

End, buying and selling, violence, defilement of the temple. קץ (190) lands at 8 (resurrection); חרב (210) at 0; חיים (68) at 12; משפט (429) at 9 (law); שממה (385) at 7 (truth, with the signature). End landing at resurrection is the place to pause: the end is not disappearance but being brought to the threshold of resurrection. Judgment sits at law, desolation on the signature. Life at 12 cannot be read as 'life is false'; it can only be read as 'life is contested on the human seat.'

- **רוח** (ruach, wind / spirit) — G=214 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=7 · composite 2×107 · Double Four · Double gate
- **כבוד** (kavod, glory) — G=32 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=5 · composite 2^5 · Double Four · Double gate
- **מזבח** (mizbeach, altar) — G=57 · m14=1 Unique · m7=1 · root=3 · composite 3×19 · Double One · Unique seat

**Reading**

Abominations in the temple: the image of jealousy, seventy elders, women weeping for Tammuz, incense to the sun. תועבה (483) and גלולים (119) both land at 7 (truth, with the signature); מזבח (57) at 1; שער (570) at 10; כבוד (32) at 4. The abomination stands on the signature — not because the idol is true, but because the idol's operating method is to occupy the seat of truth. The judgment of this chapter comes not from outside but from inside the occupied seat.

**Reading**

Six executioners, a scribe with a writing case, the mark on the forehead, the remnant. תו (406) lands at 0 (double zero); שארית (911) at 1 (unique, prime double-one); מזבח (57) at 1; כבוד (32) at 4. The mark is at zero, the remnant at the unique seat: those kept are kept not by a position but by that one mark. The forehead is the seat of identity and title (12); the mark is not in the forehead's identity but in the zero of the covenant.

## Chapter 11 — The Glory Departs, and a Stone Heart Is Exchanged for Flesh

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **רוח** (ruach, wind / spirit) — G=214 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=7 · composite 2×107 · Double Four · Double gate
- **לב** (lev, heart) — G=32 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=5 · composite 2^5 · Double Four · Double gate
- **בשר** (basar, flesh) — G=502 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=7 · composite 2×251
- **חרב** (cherev, sword) — G=210 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=3 · composite 2×3×5×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **כבוד** (kavod, glory) — G=32 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=5 · composite 2^5 · Double Four · Double gate

**Reading**

The glory departs; the prince; a new heart and a new spirit; stone exchanged for flesh. רוח (214), לב (32) and כבוד (32) all land at 4 (gate); בשר (502) at 12; אבן (53) at 11; חרב (210) at 0. New heart and new spirit both stand at the gate; the stone heart is at 11 (false light) and flesh at 12. The direction of exchange is not 'make the stone into a brighter stone' but 'return the stone's seat to the gate' — the clearest structure in the chapter.

## Chapter 12 — The Sign of Exile and the Wall Broken Through

**Chapter total:** G=97824 · m14=6 Man / Babel · m7=6 · root=3 · composite 2^5×3×1019 · 42→6 · 49→20
**Seat distribution:** 1 Unique × 5 · 12 Deceiver × 4 · 5 I/Void × 3 · 7 Truth × 3 · 9 Suffering/Law × 3 · 11 False Light × 3 · 3 One-step × 2 · 6 Man/Babel × 2 · 0 Zero × 1 · 8 Resurrection × 1 · 13 Refusal/Love × 1 (28 verses)
**Special clusters:** Double One · Unique seat: 1, 16, 17, 21, 26 (5) · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7): 5, 22, 25 (3) · Double Five · Prophet modulus: 6, 11, 20 (3) · Double Three · Instruction cluster: 9, 19 (2) · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast: 12, 28 (2) · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero: 7 (1) · Love-fire fork (m7=6 & m14=13): 18 (1)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **גולה** (golah, exile) — G=44 · m14=2 Joseph · m7=2 · root=8 · composite 2^2×11 · Double Two · Joseph seat
- **רוח** (ruach, wind / spirit) — G=214 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=7 · composite 2×107 · Double Four · Double gate
- **ירושלם** (Yerushalayim, Jerusalem) — G=586 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=1 · composite 2×293
- **חרב** (cherev, sword) — G=210 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=3 · composite 2×3×5×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **לב** (lev, heart) — G=32 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=5 · composite 2^5 · Double Four · Double gate

**Reading**

The sign of exile: baggage, digging through the wall, covering the face, eating bread with trembling. גולה (44) lands at 2 (Joseph, double two); רוח (214) at 4; ירושלם (586) at 12; חרב (210) at 0; לב (32) at 4. Exile at Joseph is the seat of 'suffering, exaltation, saving a remnant'; the sword is at zero. What the prophet performs with his body is not news but a structure already seated.

## Chapter 13 — False Prophets and the Unwhitewashed Wall

**Chapter total:** G=94546 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=1 · composite 2×41×1153 · 42→4 · 49→25
**Seat distribution:** 9 Suffering/Law × 5 · 3 One-step × 3 · 7 Truth × 3 · 0 Zero × 2 · 1 Unique × 2 · 4 Gate × 2 · 10 Costume × 2 · 2 Joseph × 1 · 5 I/Void × 1 · 6 Man/Babel × 1 · 12 Deceiver × 1 (23 verses)
**Special clusters:** I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7): 4, 5, 13 (3) · Double Three · Instruction cluster: 10, 22, 23 (3) · Double One · Unique seat: 1, 6 (2) · Double Four · Double gate: 7, 16 (2) · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero: 14, 17 (2) · Double Two · Joseph seat: 8 (1) · Double Five · Prophet modulus: 9 (1) · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast: 15 (1)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **רוח** (ruach, wind / spirit) — G=214 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=7 · composite 2×107 · Double Four · Double gate
- **לב** (lev, heart) — G=32 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=5 · composite 2^5 · Double Four · Double gate
- **ירושלם** (Yerushalayim, Jerusalem) — G=586 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=1 · composite 2×293
- **חזון** (chazon, vision) — G=71 · m14=1 Unique · m7=1 · root=8 · prime · Double One · Unique seat
- **חומה** (chomah, wall) — G=59 · m14=3 One Step from the Gate · m7=3 · root=5 · prime · Double Three · Instruction cluster
- **שקר** (sheqer, falsehood) — G=600 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=6 · composite 2^3×3×5^2

**Reading**

False prophets, the unwhitewashed wall, lying visions, women who hunt souls. חזון (71) lands at 1 (unique); חומה (59) at 3 (one step from the gate); שקר (600) at 12 (deceiver); רוח (214) and לב (32) at 4. The wall at 3 is the seat 'one step short of the gate': the wall of false peace is not a gate; it is one step away and never an entrance. Vision at the unique seat shows that a false vision is not lacking 'revelation' — it puts its own word on a seat that belongs to the One alone.

**Chapter total:** G=25222 · m14=8 Resurrection · m7=1 · root=4 · composite 2×12611 · 42→22 · 49→36
**Seat distribution:** 1 Unique × 2 · 8 Resurrection × 2 · 5 I/Void × 1 · 6 Man/Babel × 1 · 9 Suffering/Law × 1 · 12 Deceiver × 1 (8 verses)

- **אש** (esh, fire) — G=301 · m14=7 Truth · m7=0 · root=4 · composite 7×43 · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7)
- **ירושלם** (Yerushalayim, Jerusalem) — G=586 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=1 · composite 2×293
- **עץ** (etz, tree / wood) — G=160 · m14=6 Man / Babel · m7=6 · root=7 · composite 2^5×5 · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast
- **שממה** (shemamah, desolation) — G=385 · m14=7 Truth · m7=0 · root=7 · composite 5×7×11 · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7)
- **גפן** (gefen, vine) — G=133 · m14=7 Truth · m7=0 · root=7 · composite 7×19 · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7)

**Reading**

The vine, useless wood, both ends burned. אש (301) lands at 7 (signature); שממה (385) and גפן (133) also at 7; עץ (160) at 6 (man); ירושלם (586) at 12. Fire, desolation and vine share the signature: judgment is not external violence but the exposure of the useless as useless. The vine's tragedy is not that it bears no fruit but that it mistakes 'being planted' for 'being valuable.'

**Reading**

Abandoned infant, marriage covenant, ornaments, harlotry, promise of restoration. ברית (612) lands at 10 (costume); בשר (502) and ירושלם (586) at 12; עון (126) at 0; אבן (53) at 11; מצרים (380) at 2. Covenant at 10 does not mean the covenant can be abolished at will; 10 is the structural seat of what can be put on and taken off. The covenant's administration wears historical clothing, but its force is not in the clothing. The infant lifted from its blood is the chapter's first 'seat migration.'

**Reading**

Two eagles, the top of the cedar, the vine, a broken oath, Babylon. בבל (34) lands at 6 (man/Babel, double six); ארז (208) at 12 (deceiver); נשר (550) at 4 (gate); ברית (612) at 10; רוח (214) at 4. Babylon at the Babel seat is the seat of 'human engineering'; the eagle at the gate is an empire occupying the entrance; the cedar at 12 is height placed on the seat of title. The charge is not a political choice but a broken oath sworn in the divine name while turning to Egypt.

**Seat distribution:** 1 Unique × 6 · 7 Truth × 5 · 3 One-step × 4 · 10 Costume × 4 · 0 Zero × 3 · 6 Man/Babel × 3 · 13 Refusal/Love × 3 · 4 Gate × 2 · 2 Joseph × 1 · 8 Resurrection × 1 (32 verses)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **עון** (avon, iniquity) — G=126 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=9 · composite 2×3^2×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **משפט** (mishpat, judgment) — G=429 · m14=9 Suffering / Law · m7=2 · root=6 · composite 3×11×13
- **לב** (lev, heart) — G=32 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=5 · composite 2^5 · Double Four · Double gate
- **רוח** (ruach, wind / spirit) — G=214 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=7 · composite 2×107 · Double Four · Double gate
- **חיים** (chayyim, life) — G=68 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=5 · composite 2^2×17
- **צדקה** (tsedaqah, righteousness) — G=199 · m14=3 One Step from the Gate · m7=3 · root=1 · prime · Double Three · Instruction cluster

**Reading**

Each person bears his own sin; the father-son parable; turning and living. עון (126) lands at 0 (double zero); משפט (429) at 9 (law); לב (32) and רוח (214) at 4; חיים (68) at 12; צדקה (199) at 3. The sharpest point: 'life' lands at 12. Reading 12 as 'false' would contradict the chapter's plain word that the one who turns shall live; by judgment 4, seat is not value and 12 is only a coordinate. What actually moves is the ledger: from the family's joint account to each person's own.

- **מלך** (melekh, king) — G=90 · m14=6 Man / Babel · m7=6 · root=9 · composite 2×3^2×5 · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast
- **קינה** (qinah, lament) — G=165 · m14=11 False Light / Void · m7=4 · root=3 · composite 3×5×11

**Reading**

The lioness and two cubs, the vine branch and fire, a lament. מלך (90) lands at 6 (Babel); בבל (34) at 6; מצרים (380) at 2; אש (301) at 7 (signature); קינה (165) at 11; רוח (214) at 4. The lament at 11 (false light) does not make the lament false; it says the lament wears the garment of a beautiful form. King and empire are at Babel; fire is on the signature. The function of the lament is not restoration but singing out a structure that has already fallen.

- **גלולים** (gillulim, idols) — G=119 · m14=7 Truth · m7=0 · root=2 · composite 7×17 · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7)

**Reading**

Rebellion in the wilderness, Sabbaths, idols, mercy for the sake of the Name. מצרים (380) lands at 2; מדבר (246) at 8 (resurrection); ברית (612) at 10; עון (126) at 0; שבת (702) at 2; גלולים (119) at 7 (signature). The Sabbath is at Joseph and idols are on the signature: not because idols are true, but because an idol's specialty is to occupy the seat of 'Sabbath/truth.' The turn of the chapter is not the people's worthiness but the Name not being profaned.

**Seat distribution:** 1 Unique × 6 · 5 I/Void × 4 · 10 Costume × 4 · 11 False Light × 4 · 12 Deceiver × 4 · 3 One-step × 3 · 9 Suffering/Law × 3 · 2 Joseph × 2 · 4 Gate × 2 · 6 Man/Babel × 2 · 8 Resurrection × 2 · 7 Truth × 1 (37 verses)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **חרב** (cherev, sword) — G=210 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=3 · composite 2×3×5×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **בשר** (basar, flesh) — G=502 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=7 · composite 2×251

**Reading**

The sword of the LORD, the crossroads, the king of Babylon, the cut-off branch. חרב (210) lands at 0 (double zero); בשר (502) and ירושלם (586) at 12; לב (32) and רוח (214) at 4; נשיא (361) at 11. The sword at zero: the sword of judgment is not violence outside the covenant but execution inside it. The prince is at 11 (false light); flesh and city at 12. The 'divination' of the chapter is not a triumph of information; it is God using Babylon's choice at the fork.

## Chapter 22 — The Bloody City, the Furnace, and Corruption

**Chapter total:** G=109336 · m14=10 Costume / Love-Neighbor · m7=3 · root=4 · composite 2^3×79×173 · 42→10 · 49→17
**Seat distribution:** 1 Unique × 5 · 3 One-step × 5 · 7 Truth × 5 · 2 Joseph × 3 · 5 I/Void × 3 · 8 Resurrection × 2 · 11 False Light × 2 · 0 Zero × 1 · 6 Man/Babel × 1 · 9 Suffering/Law × 1 · 10 Costume × 1 · 12 Deceiver × 1 · 13 Refusal/Love × 1 (31 verses)
**Special clusters:** Double One · Unique seat: 1, 10, 17, 23, 31 (5) · Double Three · Instruction cluster: 3, 12, 20, 22, 28 (5) · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7): 11, 13, 14, 24, 27 (5) · Double Five · Prophet modulus: 2, 6, 21 (3) · Double Two · Joseph seat: 4, 9, 26 (3) · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast: 8 (1) · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero: 15 (1) · Love-fire fork (m7=6 & m14=13): 29 (1)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **דם** (dam, blood) — G=44 · m14=2 Joseph · m7=2 · root=8 · composite 2^2×11 · Double Two · Joseph seat
- **ירושלם** (Yerushalayim, Jerusalem) — G=586 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=1 · composite 2×293
- **משפט** (mishpat, judgment) — G=429 · m14=9 Suffering / Law · m7=2 · root=6 · composite 3×11×13
- **קדש** (qodesh, holy) — G=404 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=8 · composite 2^2×101
- **נביא** (navi, prophet) — G=63 · m14=7 Truth · m7=0 · root=9 · composite 3^2×7 · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7)
- **כהן** (kohen, priest) — G=75 · m14=5 I / Void · m7=5 · root=3 · composite 3×5^2 · Double Five · Prophet modulus

**Reading**

The bloody city, the furnace, the corruption of priests, prophets, princes and people. דם (44) lands at 2 (Joseph, double two); ירושלם (586) at 12; משפט (429) at 9 (law); קדש (404) at 12; נביא (63) at 7 (signature); כהן (75) at 5 (I/void). Blood at Joseph: the voice of shed blood cries from the ground. The holy at 12 shows the danger of 'holiness' turned into a title and a system. Judgment at law is the only firm coordinate in the chapter.

## Chapter 23 — Two Sisters: Oholah and Oholibah

**Chapter total:** G=180868 · m14=2 Joseph · m7=2 · root=4 · composite 2^2×103×439 · 42→16 · 49→9
**Seat distribution:** 8 Resurrection × 6 · 9 Suffering/Law × 6 · 1 Unique × 5 · 3 One-step × 5 · 6 Man/Babel × 5 · 4 Gate × 4 · 7 Truth × 4 · 12 Deceiver × 4 · 5 I/Void × 3 · 13 Refusal/Love × 3 · 2 Joseph × 2 · 11 False Light × 2 (49 verses)
**Special clusters:** Double One · Unique seat: 1, 11, 18, 30, 47 (5) · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast: 2, 4, 5, 36, 44 (5) · Double Three · Instruction cluster: 10, 13, 26, 35, 38 (5) · Double Four · Double gate: 16, 22, 23, 24 (4) · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7): 27, 29, 32, 43 (4) · Love-fire fork (m7=6 & m14=13): 6, 9, 12 (3) · Double Five · Prophet modulus: 33, 42, 49 (3) · Double Two · Joseph seat: 17, 25 (2)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **מצרים** (Mitsrayim, Egypt) — G=380 · m14=2 Joseph · m7=2 · root=2 · composite 2^2×5×19 · Double Two · Joseph seat
- **בשר** (basar, flesh) — G=502 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=7 · composite 2×251
- **בבל** (Bavel, Babylon) — G=34 · m14=6 Man / Babel · m7=6 · root=7 · composite 2×17 · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast
- **חרב** (cherev, sword) — G=210 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=3 · composite 2×3×5×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **משפט** (mishpat, judgment) — G=429 · m14=9 Suffering / Law · m7=2 · root=6 · composite 3×11×13
- **אשה** (ishah, woman) — G=306 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=9 · composite 2×3^2×17
- **אחות** (achot, sister) — G=415 · m14=9 Suffering / Law · m7=2 · root=1 · composite 5×83
- **ירושלם** (Yerushalayim, Jerusalem) — G=586 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=1 · composite 2×293

**Reading**

Two sisters, Oholah and Oholibah, lovers from Egypt, Assyria and Babylon, and judgment. מצרים (380) lands at 2; בשר (502), אשה (306) and ירושלם (586) at 12; בבל (34) at 6 (Babel, double six); חרב (210) at 0; משפט (429) at 9; אחות (415) at 9. Body, woman and city share 12 — the structure of identity occupied by title and desire; the sisters share 9 (law), where rivalry and judgment sit together. The imperial lovers are at Babel; the sword is at zero. The chapter writes political alliance as bodily relation precisely to make the seats visible.

## Chapter 24 — The Rusty Pot, and a Wife's Death as a Sign

**Chapter total:** G=96517 · m14=1 Unique · m7=1 · root=1 · prime · 42→1 · 49→36
**Seat distribution:** 2 Joseph × 4 · 1 Unique × 3 · 5 I/Void × 3 · 12 Deceiver × 3 · 13 Refusal/Love × 3 · 0 Zero × 2 · 4 Gate × 2 · 10 Costume × 2 · 11 False Light × 2 · 3 One-step × 1 · 7 Truth × 1 · 8 Resurrection × 1 (27 verses)
**Special clusters:** Double Two · Joseph seat: 11, 16, 22, 26 (4) · Love-fire fork (m7=6 & m14=13): 2, 6, 8 (3) · Double One · Unique seat: 7, 15, 20 (3) · Double Five · Prophet modulus: 9, 12, 23 (3) · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero: 4, 21 (2) · Double Four · Double gate: 13, 19 (2) · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7): 5 (1) · Double Three · Instruction cluster: 25 (1)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **עצם** (etzem, bone) — G=200 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=2 · composite 2^3×5^2 · Double Four · Double gate
- **בשר** (basar, flesh) — G=502 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=7 · composite 2×251
- **ירושלם** (Yerushalayim, Jerusalem) — G=586 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=1 · composite 2×293
- **חרב** (cherev, sword) — G=210 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=3 · composite 2×3×5×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **אש** (esh, fire) — G=301 · m14=7 Truth · m7=0 · root=4 · composite 7×43 · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7)

**Reading**

The rusty pot, the siege, the wife's death as a sign, silence. עצם (200) lands at 4 (gate, double four); בשר (502) and ירושלם (586) at 12; חרב (210) at 0; אש (301) at 7 (signature). Bone/self at the gate, flesh at 12: the meat in the pot is not life, only flesh being boiled. The wife's death and the prophet's silence push the 'sign' to its limit — this chapter's decoding does not explain suffering; it admits that some seats can only be occupied by silence.

**Reading**

Ammon, Moab, Edom and Philistia judged. חרב (210) and עון (126) land at 0 (double zero); גולה (44) at 2; שארית (911) at 1 (unique); שממה (385) at 7; נקמה (195) at 13 (love seat, love-fire fork). Vengeance at 13 shares a seat with love: this is a direct instance of the love-fire fork. It does not say vengeance is love; it says vengeance and love share one seat and are distinguished only by oath and fruit.

**Reading**

The judgment of Tyre, the prince in the sea, the wall, the merchants, the deep. צור (296) lands at 2 (Joseph); חומה (59) at 3 (one step from the gate); בבל (34) at 6; חיים (68) at 12; חרב (210) at 0. Tyre (rock) is at Joseph; the wall is at the seat 'one step short of the gate': the self-sufficient city treats its wall as a gate and remains one step away. Life again at 12 — by judgment 4, not 'life is false.'

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **לב** (lev, heart) — G=32 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=5 · composite 2^5 · Double Four · Double gate
- **רוח** (ruach, wind / spirit) — G=214 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=7 · composite 2×107 · Double Four · Double gate

**Reading**

The lament of Tyre, the beautiful ship, cargo, sinking. צור (296) lands at 2; אבן (53) at 11; מצרים (380) at 2; חיל (48) at 6; לב (32) and רוח (214) at 4. The ship's body is described with the gate vocabulary of heart and spirit, while cargo and wealth are at Babel; the stones (ballast, gems) are at false light. The lament breaks 'beauty' down into a priced manifest — the sharpest structure in the chapter.

**Reading**

The prince of Tyre, the anointed cherub, pride, the fall; Sidon judged. גאון (60) lands at 4 (gate); צור (296) at 2; אבן (53) at 11; קדש (404) at 12; חרב (210) at 0; לב (32) at 4. Pride at the gate is the most sobering point: pride does not stand outside the door; it stands in the doorway and takes itself for the door. The anointed cherub is a gate-seat creature; the fall is not loss of power but exchanging 'guarding the gate' for 'being the gate.'

- **מלך** (melekh, king) — G=90 · m14=6 Man / Babel · m7=6 · root=9 · composite 2×3^2×5 · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast

**Reading**

Egypt, Pharaoh as the great dragon, the Nile, forty years of desolation. מצרים (380) lands at 2 (Joseph); תנין (510) at 6 (Babel); יאור (217) at 7 (truth, with the signature); מלך (90) and בבל (34) at 6; חרב (210) at 0. Egypt at Joseph (historically a refuge), dragon and king at Babel, the Nile on the signature: the created water treated as the source of life is the idol structure of the chapter.

**Reading**

Assyria as a cedar, many waters, Eden, descent to Sheol. ארז (208) lands at 12 (deceiver); מים (90) at 6 (man); כבוד (32) at 4; מצרים (380) at 2; שאול (337) at 1 (unique); חרב (210) at 0. The cedar at 12 (title/identity), water at 6 (man), Sheol at 1 (unique): height, depth and uniqueness are put on three different seats. The irony: the tallest tree is placed on the seat of identity, while the deepest pit is placed on the seat of the One.

**Reading**

The lament of Egypt, descent to the pit, the uncircumcised, the beds of the nations. מצרים (380) lands at 2; חיים (68) at 12; שאול (337) at 1; קבר (302) at 8 (resurrection); לב (32) at 4; חרב (210) at 0. The grave at resurrection, Sheol at the unique seat: death is not a synonym for the end but is set opposite resurrection. Life at 12 repeats the reminder: a seat is not a verdict.

**Reading**

The watchman, the fall of Jerusalem, ears that hear but do not do, each bearing his own. צפה (175) lands at 7 (signature); שוב (308) at 0 (double zero); רשע (570) at 10; משפט (429) at 9; חיים (68) at 12; חרב (210) at 0. Turning (שוב) at zero is the cleanest structure in the book: turning is returning to the covenant's starting point. The watchman stands on the signature, while the hearers remain in the crack between hearing and doing.

## Chapter 34 — Shepherds Judged and the Good Shepherd

**Chapter total:** G=125039 · m14=5 I / Void · m7=5 · root=2 · composite 19×6581 · 42→5 · 49→40
**Seat distribution:** 7 Truth × 4 · 9 Suffering/Law × 4 · 3 One-step × 3 · 4 Gate × 3 · 0 Zero × 2 · 1 Unique × 2 · 5 I/Void × 2 · 6 Man/Babel × 2 · 11 False Light × 2 · 12 Deceiver × 2 · 13 Refusal/Love × 2 · 2 Joseph × 1 · 8 Resurrection × 1 · 10 Costume × 1 (31 verses)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **ברית** (berit, covenant) — G=612 · m14=10 Costume / Love-Neighbor · m7=3 · root=9 · composite 2^2×3^2×17
- **משפט** (mishpat, judgment) — G=429 · m14=9 Suffering / Law · m7=2 · root=6 · composite 3×11×13
- **נשיא** (nasi, prince / leader) — G=361 · m14=11 False Light / Void · m7=4 · root=1 · composite 19^2
- **צאן** (tson, flock) — G=141 · m14=1 Unique · m7=1 · root=6 · composite 3×47 · Double One · Unique seat
- **רועה** (ro'eh, shepherd) — G=281 · m14=1 Unique · m7=1 · root=2 · prime · Double One · Unique seat
- **רפא** (rapha, to heal) — G=281 · m14=1 Unique · m7=1 · root=2 · prime · Double One · Unique seat

**Reading**

Shepherds judged, the lost sheep brought back, the good shepherd, a covenant of peace. רועה (281) and רפא (281) both land at 1 (unique; 281 is prime, double one); צאן (141 = 3×47) also lands at 1 but is a composite double-one; ברית (612) at 10; משפט (429) at 9; נשיא (361) at 11. Shepherd and healing share the value 281, both prime and both double-one — the most concentrated pastoral cluster in the book. The flock, though composite, cannot be read literally as 'usurping' — exactly what judgment 4 blocks.

## Chapter 35 — Mount Seir and Edom

**Chapter total:** G=52911 · m14=5 I / Void · m7=5 · root=9 · composite 3^2×5879 · 42→33 · 49→40
**Seat distribution:** 1 Unique × 4 · 6 Man/Babel × 3 · 2 Joseph × 2 · 4 Gate × 2 · 8 Resurrection × 2 · 12 Deceiver × 1 · 13 Refusal/Love × 1 (15 verses)
**Special clusters:** Double One · Unique seat: 1, 6, 7, 13 (4) · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast: 2, 12, 14 (3) · Double Four · Double gate: 3, 8 (2) · Double Two · Joseph seat: 4, 10 (2) · Love-fire fork (m7=6 & m14=13): 11 (1)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **שממה** (shemamah, desolation) — G=385 · m14=7 Truth · m7=0 · root=7 · composite 5×7×11 · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7)
- **חרב** (cherev, sword) — G=210 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=3 · composite 2×3×5×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **דם** (dam, blood) — G=44 · m14=2 Joseph · m7=2 · root=8 · composite 2^2×11 · Double Two · Joseph seat
- **עון** (avon, iniquity) — G=126 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=9 · composite 2×3^2×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **מות** (mavet, death / to die) — G=446 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=5 · composite 2×223
- **נחלה** (nachalah, inheritance) — G=93 · m14=9 Suffering / Law · m7=2 · root=3 · composite 3×31

**Reading**

Mount Seir and Edom: perpetual enmity, bloodshed, desolation, inheritance. שממה (385) lands at 7 (signature); חרב (210) and עון (126) at 0; דם (44) at 2; מות (446) at 12; נחלה (93) at 9. Desolation stands on the signature: Edom's end is not 'being defeated' but 'being shown to be desolation.' Inheritance at the seat of law shows that the land dispute is a dispute over who inherits the covenant.

**Reading**

The mountains of Israel restored, cleansing, a new heart and new spirit, stone exchanged for flesh, ruins rebuilt. לב (32), רוח (214) and טהר (214) all land at 4 (gate); בשר (502) at 12; אבן (53) at 11; שארית (911) at 1. Heart, spirit and cleansing share the gate — the clearest 'renewal cluster' in the book; the stone heart is at 11 (false light) and flesh at 12. Renewal does not polish the stone; it returns the stone's seat to the gate.

## Chapter 37 — The Dry Bones and the Two Sticks

**Chapter total:** G=108138 · m14=2 Joseph · m7=2 · root=3 · composite 2×3×67×269 · 42→30 · 49→44
**Seat distribution:** 6 Man/Babel × 4 · 11 False Light × 4 · 1 Unique × 3 · 5 I/Void × 3 · 8 Resurrection × 3 · 13 Refusal/Love × 3 · 7 Truth × 2 · 10 Costume × 2 · 12 Deceiver × 2 · 2 Joseph × 1 · 3 One-step × 1 (28 verses)
**Special clusters:** Double Six · Battlefield/Beast: 8, 16, 21, 28 (4) · Double Five · Prophet modulus: 3, 9, 26 (3) · Double One · Unique seat: 4, 10, 15 (3) · Love-fire fork (m7=6 & m14=13): 14, 22, 23 (3) · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7): 1, 11 (2) · Double Three · Instruction cluster: 24 (1) · Double Two · Joseph seat: 27 (1)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **רוח** (ruach, wind / spirit) — G=214 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=7 · composite 2×107 · Double Four · Double gate
- **בשר** (basar, flesh) — G=502 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=7 · composite 2×251
- **עצם** (etzem, bone) — G=200 · m14=4 Gate · m7=4 · root=2 · composite 2^3×5^2 · Double Four · Double gate
- **ברית** (berit, covenant) — G=612 · m14=10 Costume / Love-Neighbor · m7=3 · root=9 · composite 2^2×3^2×17
- **תחיה** (techiyah, resurrection) — G=423 · m14=3 One Step from the Gate · m7=3 · root=9 · composite 3^2×47 · Double Three · Instruction cluster
- **שבט** (shevet, tribe / rod) — G=311 · m14=3 One Step from the Gate · m7=3 · root=5 · prime · Double Three · Instruction cluster

**Reading**

Dry bones, resurrection, two sticks, one nation and one shepherd. רוח (214) and עצם (200) land at 4 (gate); שבט (311) at 3 (one step from the gate); בשר (502) at 12; ברית (612) at 10; תחיה (423) at 3. Bones and wind are at the gate, but tribe and resurrection are at 3: resurrection is not a finished state but a promise 'one step from the gate'; when the wind blows, the gate opens. Flesh at 12 remains the contested carrier of life.

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **חרב** (cherev, sword) — G=210 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=3 · composite 2×3×5×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **נשיא** (nasi, prince / leader) — G=361 · m14=11 False Light / Void · m7=4 · root=1 · composite 19^2
- **גוג** (Gog, Gog) — G=12 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=3 · composite 2^2×3

- **סוס** (sus, horse) — G=126 · m14=0 Zero / Davidic Covenant · m7=0 · root=9 · composite 2×3^2×7 · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero
- **קשת** (qeshet, bow) — G=800 · m14=2 Joseph · m7=2 · root=8 · composite 2^5×5^2 · Double Two · Joseph seat

**Reading**

Gog, Magog, the latter-day coalition, bow and horses, the LORD's intervention. גוג (12) lands at 12 (deceiver); מגוג (52) at 10 (costume); נשיא (361) at 11 (false light); חרב (210) at 0; סוס (126) at 0 (double zero); קשת (800) at 2 (Joseph). Gog, Magog and the prince form a counterfeit triad 12/10/11: deception, costume, false light. Horses at zero: the war-horse's strength returns to the covenant's starting point and is not in its own power. This marks a seat structure, not a person; identification must be left to oath and fruit.

**Reading**

The defeat of Gog, the birds' feast, the cleansing of the land, the outpouring of the Spirit. גוג (12) lands at 12; מגוג (52) at 10; בשר (502) at 12; עצם (200) at 4; עוף (156) at 2 (Joseph); חרב (210) at 0. The birds are at Joseph: the supply of the feast comes from outside; even the flesh-eating birds are summoned. Gog is still at 12, but this time seat 12 is publicly executed.

**Reading**

The new temple: east gate, outer court, chambers, altar, measuring reed. שער (570) lands at 10 (costume); עצם (200) and חצר (298) at 4 (gate); מזבח (57) and קנה (155) at 1 (unique); בשר (502) at 12. The gate at 10 is the seat of 'the garment of authority': the gate is not the gate itself but the qualification for entry. Altar and reed at the unique seat: the measure of the new house is not human taste but the one measure.

## Chapter 45 — The Holy District, the Prince, and the Offerings

**Chapter total:** G=121400 · m14=6 Man / Babel · m7=6 · root=8 · composite 2^3×5^2×607 · 42→20 · 49→27
**Seat distribution:** 8 Resurrection × 4 · 9 Suffering/Law × 3 · 10 Costume × 3 · 12 Deceiver × 3 · 5 I/Void × 2 · 11 False Light × 2 · 13 Refusal/Love × 2 · 0 Zero × 1 · 1 Unique × 1 · 2 Joseph × 1 · 3 One-step × 1 · 6 Man/Babel × 1 · 7 Truth × 1 (25 verses)
**Special clusters:** Double Five · Prophet modulus: 2, 20 (2) · Love-fire fork (m7=6 & m14=13): 5, 7 (2) · Double Zero · Covenant/Zero: 1 (1) · Double One · Unique seat: 6 (1) · Double Two · Joseph seat: 10 (1) · Double Three · Instruction cluster: 14 (1) · Double Six · Battlefield/Beast: 16 (1) · I AM signature (m7=0 & m14=7): 18 (1)

**Thematic words** (selected for the chapter's theme; the value is the standard gematria of the word form, not an occurrence count):

- **מקדש** (miqdash, sanctuary) — G=444 · m14=10 Costume / Love-Neighbor · m7=3 · root=3 · composite 2^2×3×37
- **קדש** (qodesh, holy) — G=404 · m14=12 Deceiver · m7=5 · root=8 · composite 2^2×101
- **נשיא** (nasi, prince / leader) — G=361 · m14=11 False Light / Void · m7=4 · root=1 · composite 19^2
- **מזבח** (mizbeach, altar) — G=57 · m14=1 Unique · m7=1 · root=3 · composite 3×19 · Double One · Unique seat
- **נחלה** (nachalah, inheritance) — G=93 · m14=9 Suffering / Law · m7=2 · root=3 · composite 3×31
- **מנחה** (minchah, grain offering) — G=103 · m14=5 I / Void · m7=5 · root=4 · prime · Double Five · Prophet modulus

**Reading**

The holy district, the prince's portion, the offerings, the feasts, the inheritance. מקדש (444) lands at 10; קדש (404) at 12; נשיא (361) at 11 (false light); מזבח (57) at 1; נחלה (93) at 9; מנחה (103) at 5 (I/void). The prince at 11 and the altar at 1: the 'light' of authority and the 'uniqueness' of the altar are separated — the chapter's most important political-sacrificial boundary.

The river flowing from the temple gate, healing water, trees, fish, the border. נחל (88) lands at 4 (gate); רפא (281) at 1 (unique); מים (90) at 6 (man); מקדש (444) at 10; קדש (404) at 12; מזבח (57) at 1. The river flows from the gate, healing is at the unique seat, water is at the human seat: the water of life does not spring from the human seat; it passes through the gate, is healed at the unique seat, and then reaches the places of men.

**Reading**

The allotments of the twelve tribes, the holy city, its gates, 'The LORD is there.' שבט (311) lands at 3 (one step from the gate); שער (570) at 10; קדש (404) at 12; מקדש (444) at 10; נחלה (93) at 9; יובל (48) at 6. The tribes are at 'one step from the gate': the allotment is the promise fulfilled, but entry still passes through the gate. The final name, יהוה שמה ('the LORD is there'), totals 371, lands at 7 (truth) and carries the I AM signature (m7=0) — the book ends on the signature of truth, not on the dimensions of any city.

## 6. Structural observations

1. Gate cluster (m14=4, double four): רוח 214, לב 32, כבוד 32, עצם 200, כרוב 228, חצר 298, נחל 88, טהר 214. Wind/spirit, heart, glory, bones, cherubim, court, river and cleansing share one gate coordinate. Tribe (שבט 311) lands at 3, not at the gate: a tribe must still take one step to enter its inheritance.
2. Zero cluster (m14=0, double zero): חרב 210, עון 126, מצור 336, שוב 308, תו 406, זהב 14. Sword, iniquity, siege, turning, mark and gold all land at the covenant's starting point: judgment and return are never outside the covenant.
3. Truth/signature cluster (m14=7, m7=0): אש 301, נביא 63, צפה 175, שממה 385, גפן 133, יאור 217. Fire, prophet, watchman, desolation, vine and Nile all carry the I AM signature. תועבה 483 and גלולים 119 also land at 7: an idol occupying the seat of truth is the structural definition of 'counterfeit,' not evidence that the idol is true. (תחיה 423 lands at 3, not 7; the word 'resurrection' does not share the signature seat and must not be conflated with it.)
4. Love-fire fork (m14=13, m7=6): ישועה 391 (salvation) and נקמה 195 (vengeance) share a seat. Love and fire share a seat; the distinction is not in the seat but in oath and fruit.
5. Same value, different meaning: גאל 34 (redeem) and בבל 34 (Babylon) share both the value and seat 6 (double six); חטא 18 (sin) and חי 18 (live) share the value and seat 4 (double four). These are two instances in Ezekiel of the source text's 'same number, opposite meaning' theme; they are structural comparisons only and cannot be used to infer identity.
6. Seat 12 is not 'for bad things only': בשר 502, ירושלם 586, קדש 404, חיים 68 and מות 446 all land at 12. Reading 12 as 'false' would make life false — 'life at 12' is exactly the reading judgment 4 blocks.
7. Pastoral cluster: רועה 281 and רפא 281 are both prime and both land at 1 (double one); צאן 141 = 3×47 also lands at 1 but is composite. Prime and composite stand side by side in one cluster: 'double one' is not a moral seal.
8. The final name: יהוה שמה ('the LORD is there') = 26 + 345 = 371, m14=7, m7=0 — the truth seat, with the I AM signature. The book ends on the signature of truth, not on the dimensions of any city.

## 7. Framework limits

What this edition can do: place the text on the seats by the stated rules, mark seat, cluster, prime/composite and the 42/49 remainders, and leave every number recomputable.

What it cannot do: it cannot infer from a seat that the revelation is true; it cannot use a seat to identify any person; it cannot declare on God's behalf 'who is.' If a landing is read as "therefore the revelation is verified," rule 1 (decoding is lossy) has been crossed. If a seat-12 landing is read as "therefore it is false," rule 2 (seat is not value) has been crossed. The end of decoding is not a verdict; it is to return the text to the text and judgment to God.
